7//! Typed, generator-only definitions for authored validation steps.
8//!
9//! This module is private to `hermit-manifest-plan`: runtime validation cannot
10//! import it and consumes only `ci/dag/validate.json`. The maintenance generator
11//! combines these authored steps with the corpus-derived partitions, then emits
12//! that one committed DAG. Keeping the source typed and independent makes an
13//! arbitrary command, dependency, or cap edit in the generated file stale.
14
15use std::collections::BTreeMap;
16
17use dagrun::model::CmdType;
18use dagrun::model::DagConfig;
19use dagrun::model::DagManifest;
20use dagrun::model::ResourceHint;
21use dagrun::model::ResultManifest;
22use dagrun::model::Step;
23use dagrun::model::StepClass;
24use dagrun::model::StructuredTestResultsManifest;
25
26pub(super) const RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_COMMAND: &str = r########"export PATH="$PWD/ci/rust-script-bin:$PATH"; export HERMIT_RUST_SCRIPT_ARTIFACT_ROOT="$PWD/target/ci/rust-scripts"; export HERMIT_PREBUILT_RUST_SCRIPTS_REQUIRED=1; ./ci/prepare-rust-scripts.sh"########;
27const RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_DESCRIPTION: &str = r########"DISCOVERED PRODUCER 2026-08-31: every tracked rust-script entrypoint is generated as a Cargo package, checked with the existing clippy policy, built as a release executable, and, when it carries tests, built as a test harness. The outputs are copied into target/ci/rust-scripts after checkout and pin verification, and all compiling consumers wait for this node. Repository graph commands resolve rust-script through ci/rust-script-bin, which executes only the published binaries and never invokes Cargo; sources owned by external operational tooling remain that tooling's responsibility. Two concurrent rust-script --force probes sharing one cache measured a real Cargo build-directory lock wait; separate caches removed the wait but duplicated compilation. Keeping one writer and read-only consumers makes compilation placement deterministic without creating per-node target directories. RESOURCE BOUNDS measured 2026-09-14 at Hermit 7b8be60708c29b4926f36f06be8793523841d6ba: the old 2-GiB hard cap killed the cold width-8 producer in run 1812 and in a focused width-4 control. With the unchanged 28-entrypoint/17-harness workload and width 8, three ordinary cold runs with memory.max=6442450944 bytes passed with cgroup peaks 3836551168, 3845783552, and 3841757184 bytes; three pinned-root cold runs under that same memory.max passed with peaks 3803856896, 3721408512, and 3691466752 bytes. All six recorded memory.events max=0, oom=0, and oom_kill=0. The 4-GiB baseline rounds above the measured high-water mark for scheduler accounting; the 6-GiB hard cap adds more than 2.4 GiB of runaway headroom while fitting the project's 8-GiB minimum runner. preferred_inner_jobs=8 remains paired with CARGO_BUILD_JOBS so the scheduler reserves the same eight-CPU width Cargo actually uses. The ordinary producer's 900-second wall bound is the width-8 wall equivalent of its unchanged 7200-second CPU bound. Quick and super use a distinct 1200-second CPU budget and zero duration estimate: 1200 is the next project 300-second bucket above 1.5 times the largest measured 737.730-second CPU sample. The Step constructor orders these as wall, CPU, then memory."########;

31pub(super) const RUST_SCRIPT_PRODUCER_INNER_JOBS: i64 = 8;

34/// The controlled writer a static validation step invokes.
35///
36/// This is authored metadata, not command-string inference. The generator audits
37/// the command independently so adding or removing a writer cannot silently leave
38/// this declaration stale.
39#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, Ord, PartialEq, PartialOrd)]
40pub(super) enum StructuredResultProducerKind {
41    Nextest,
42    TestHarness,
43    BackendParity,
44    Envelope,
45    Applications,
46}
47
48impl StructuredResultProducerKind {
49    pub(super) const ALL: [Self; 5] = [
50        Self::Nextest,
51        Self::TestHarness,
52        Self::BackendParity,
53        Self::Envelope,
54        Self::Applications,
55    ];
56
57    pub(super) const fn command_marker(self) -> &'static str {
58        match self {
59            Self::Nextest => "run-nextest-counted.sh",
60            Self::TestHarness => "target/debug/test-harness run",
61            Self::BackendParity => "tests/backend-parity/run_matrix.py",
62            Self::Envelope => "write-structured-test-counts.sh",
63            Self::Applications => "tests/e2e/lib/applications/run_all.sh",
64        }
65    }
66
67    pub(super) const fn tags(self) -> &'static [&'static str] {
68        match self {
69            Self::Nextest => NEXTEST_RESULT_PRODUCERS,
70            Self::TestHarness => TEST_HARNESS_RESULT_PRODUCERS,
71            Self::BackendParity => BACKEND_PARITY_RESULT_PRODUCERS,
72            Self::Envelope => ENVELOPE_RESULT_PRODUCERS,
73            Self::Applications => APPLICATION_RESULT_PRODUCERS,
74        }
75    }
76}

247
248pub(super) fn structured_result_producer_kind(tag: &str) -> Option<StructuredResultProducerKind> {
249    for (tags, kind) in [
250        (
251            NEXTEST_RESULT_PRODUCERS,
252            StructuredResultProducerKind::Nextest,
253        ),
254        (
255            TEST_HARNESS_RESULT_PRODUCERS,
256            StructuredResultProducerKind::TestHarness,
257        ),
258        (
259            BACKEND_PARITY_RESULT_PRODUCERS,
260            StructuredResultProducerKind::BackendParity,
261        ),
262        (
263            ENVELOPE_RESULT_PRODUCERS,
264            StructuredResultProducerKind::Envelope,
265        ),
266        (
267            APPLICATION_RESULT_PRODUCERS,
268            StructuredResultProducerKind::Applications,
269        ),
270    ] {
271        if tags.contains(&tag) {
272            return Some(kind);
273        }
274    }
275    None
276}
277
278fn expected_nextest_count(tag: &str) -> Option<u64> {
279    NEXTEST_EXPECTED_COUNTS
280        .iter()
281        .find_map(|(candidate, count)| (*candidate == tag).then_some(*count))
282}
283
284#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
285struct ManifestSpec {
286    lane: &'static str,
287    category: &'static str,
288    test: Option<&'static str>,
289    mode: Option<&'static str>,
290    backend: Option<&'static str>,
291}
292
293impl ManifestSpec {
294    fn materialize(self) -> DagManifest {
295        DagManifest {
296            lane: self.lane.into(),
297            category: self.category.into(),
298            test: self.test.map(Into::into),
299            mode: self.mode.map(Into::into),
300            backend: self.backend.map(Into::into),
301        }
302    }
303}
304
305#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
306struct HintSpec {
307    resources: &'static [(&'static str, i64)],
308    est_duration_s: f64,
309    rss_baseline_bytes: Option<i64>,
310    hard_mem_max_bytes: Option<i64>,
311    classification: StepClass,
312    preferred_inner_jobs: Option<i64>,
313    measured_effective_cores: Option<f64>,
314    measured_cpu_utilization: Option<f64>,
315}
316
317impl HintSpec {
318    fn materialize(self) -> ResourceHint {
319        ResourceHint {
320            resources: self
321                .resources
322                .iter()
323                .map(|(name, demand)| ((*name).into(), *demand))
324                .collect(),
325            est_duration_s: self.est_duration_s,
326            rss_baseline_bytes: self.rss_baseline_bytes,
327            rss_baseline_inner_jobs: None,
328            hard_mem_max_bytes: self.hard_mem_max_bytes,
329            classification: self.classification,
330            preferred_inner_jobs: self.preferred_inner_jobs,
331            measured_effective_cores: self.measured_effective_cores,
332            measured_cpu_utilization: self.measured_cpu_utilization,
333        }
334    }
335}
336
337#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
338struct StaticStepSpec {
339    group: &'static str,
340    job: &'static str,
341    desc: &'static str,
342    description: &'static str,
343    labels: &'static [&'static str],
344    cmd: &'static str,
345    cmdtype: CmdType,
346    manifest: Option<ManifestSpec>,
347    integration_test_binaries: Option<&'static [&'static str]>,
348    deps: &'static [&'static str],
349    env: &'static [(&'static str, &'static str)],
350    hint: HintSpec,
351    networkonly: bool,
352    engine_only: bool,
353    timeout: i64,
354    cpu_timeout: i64,
355    jobs_flag: Option<&'static str>,
356    jobs_env: Option<&'static str>,
357}
358
359impl StaticStepSpec {
360    fn materialize(self) -> Step {
361        let tag = format!("{}.{}", self.group, self.job);
362        let producer = structured_result_producer_kind(&tag);
363        let mut env = self
364            .env
365            .iter()
366            .map(|(name, value)| ((*name).into(), (*value).into()))
367            .collect::<BTreeMap<String, String>>();
368        if let Some(expected) = expected_nextest_count(&tag) {
369            assert_eq!(
370                producer,
371                Some(StructuredResultProducerKind::Nextest),
372                "{tag} has an expected Nextest count but is not a declared Nextest producer"
373            );
374            assert!(
375                env.insert("NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED".into(), expected.to_string())
376                    .is_none(),
377                "{tag} declares NEXTEST_EXPECTED_EXECUTED twice"
378            );
379        }
380        if producer == Some(StructuredResultProducerKind::Nextest)
381            || self.cmd.contains("nextest-binaries.rs executable ")
382        {
383            let selection = crate::nextest_build_selections::for_step(&tag)
384                .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{tag} has no declared Cargo build selection"));
385            assert!(
386                env.insert(
387                    crate::nextest_binaries::SELECTION_ENV.into(),
388                    serde_json::to_string(selection).expect("string list is serializable"),
389                )
390                .is_none()
391            );
392            assert!(
393                env.insert(crate::nextest_binaries::REQUIRED_ENV.into(), "1".into())
394                    .is_none()
395            );
396        }
397        Step {
398            group: self.group.into(),
399            job: self.job.into(),
400            desc: self.desc.into(),
401            description: self.description.into(),
402            labels: self.labels.iter().map(|value| (*value).into()).collect(),
403            cmd: self.cmd.into(),
404            cmdtype: self.cmdtype,
405            manifest: self.manifest.map(ManifestSpec::materialize),
406            integration_test_binaries: self
407                .integration_test_binaries
408                .map(|values| values.iter().map(|value| (*value).into()).collect()),
409            result_manifests: Some(
410                producer
411                    .map(|_| {
412                        vec![ResultManifest::StructuredTestResults(
413                            StructuredTestResultsManifest::current(tag.clone()),
414                        )]
415                    })
416                    .unwrap_or_default(),
417            ),
418            deps: self.deps.iter().map(|value| (*value).into()).collect(),
419            env,
420            hint: self.hint.materialize(),
421            networkonly: self.networkonly,
422            engine_only: self.engine_only,
423            timeout: self.timeout,
424            cpu_timeout: self.cpu_timeout,
425            jobs_flag: self.jobs_flag.map(Into::into),
426            jobs_env: self.jobs_env.map(Into::into),
427            skip_reason: None,
428            write_domains: None,
429            write_domain_guarantee: None,
430            explains: Vec::new(),
431            fail_fast_family: PMU_MEMORY_FAILURE_FAMILY_MEMBERS
432                .contains(&tag.as_str())
433                .then(|| PMU_MEMORY_FAILURE_FAMILY.into()),
434        }
435    }
436}
437
438pub(super) fn config() -> DagConfig {
439    let mut steps = STATIC_STEPS
440        .iter()
441        .copied()
442        .map(StaticStepSpec::materialize)
443        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
444    for (parent, job, description, command) in [
445        (
446            "cli",
447            "isolated_dbt_workdir",
448            "Verify sequential and concurrent physical DBT working directories and retain the existing blocked-input failure control. The exact selected tests require the pinned-root marker, prepared CLI executable, canonical INFO and IO-buffer comparison, and unchanged per-test CPU/wall policy. Retained files additionally inherit a 64 MiB per-file limit; a cap hit remains failure.",
449            concat!(
450                "export PATH=\"$PWD/ci/rust-script-bin:$PATH\"; export HERMIT_RUST_SCRIPT_ARTIFACT_ROOT=\"$PWD/target/ci/rust-scripts\"; export HERMIT_PREBUILT_RUST_SCRIPTS_REQUIRED=1; ",
451                "test \"${HERMIT_E2E_EMPTY_WORKDIR:-}\" = /test && ",
452                "prlimit --fsize=67108864:67108864 -- ./ci/run-with-reverie-dbt-budget.sh ./ci/run-nextest-counted.sh ",
453                "${CI:+--profile ci} -p hermit --features third-party-backends --test cli ",
454                "-E 'test(=run_dbt_verifies_fresh_physical_workdirs) | test(=run_dbt_strict_returns_with_blocked_stdin_source)' -- --include-ignored"
455            ),
456        ),
457        (
458            "regular_crates",
459            "isolated_detcore_workdir",
460            "Execute the marked in-process testutils control through its existing prepared regular-crates selection. Require exactly one terminal test covering two generic Tool runs and two Detcore strict-log repetitions, with the original CPU/wall policy and a 64 MiB inherited per-file limit.",
461            concat!(
462                "export PATH=\"$PWD/ci/rust-script-bin:$PATH\"; export HERMIT_RUST_SCRIPT_ARTIFACT_ROOT=\"$PWD/target/ci/rust-scripts\"; export HERMIT_PREBUILT_RUST_SCRIPTS_REQUIRED=1; ",
463                "test \"${HERMIT_E2E_EMPTY_WORKDIR:-}\" = /test && ",
464                "prlimit --fsize=67108864:67108864 -- ./ci/run-with-reverie-dbt-budget.sh ./ci/run-nextest-counted.sh ",
465                "${CI:+--profile ci} --workspace --exclude hermit-detcore --exclude hermit --exclude hermetic_infra_hermit_flaky-tests ",
466                "-E 'package(=detcore-testutils) & test(=tests::isolated_workdir_reaches_the_in_process_guest_when_requested)'"
467            ),
468        ),
469    ] {
470        let parent = *STATIC_STEPS
471            .iter()
472            .find(|step| step.group == "test" && step.job == parent)
473            .unwrap();
474        steps.push(
475            StaticStepSpec {
476                job,
477                desc: description,
478                description,
479                labels: &["full", "portable"],
480                cmd: command,
481                hint: HintSpec {
482                    preferred_inner_jobs: Some(1),
483                    ..parent.hint
484                },
485                jobs_flag: Some(""),
486                jobs_env: Some("NEXTEST_TEST_THREADS"),
487                ..parent
488            }
489            .materialize(),
490        );
491    }
492    DagConfig {
493        description: "Hermit validation superset; select quick, portable, hosted-portable, full, super, privileged, or hosted-privileged by step label".into(),
494        default_step_timeout: 600,
495        resource_caps: BTreeMap::from([("manifest_guest".into(), 8), ("integration_test_binaries.cli".into(), 1), ("integration_test_binaries.hermit_modes".into(), 1)]),
496        steps,
497        ..DagConfig::default()
498    }
499}

The sibling test.detcore_unit (-p hermit-detcore --lib --bins) has never carried it, so the choice is selective. Hermit's INTEGRATION tests get the same serialization by a different route -- .config/nextest.toml caps the test-group hermit-serialized at max-threads 1 over filter package(=hermit) & kind(=test); --lib and --bins are NOT kind(=test), which is why this node needs the flag instead. That file states the reason at lines 30-32: the hermit test binaries 'use process-local mutexes to serialize Hermit executions', and several tests here do execute Hermit (for example run::detects_symlink_resolution_through_implicit_mounts and the verify::tests::* family). MEASURED COST OF THE PIN: 1.93x. Two replicates each at a6b0c37648df on the host recorded for this measurement in docs/TESTING_ENVIRONMENTS.md under Named measurement hosts, -j1 gave 27.0s and 27.3s against -j16 at 14.4s and 13.7s. WARNING FOR WHOEVER MEASURES THIS NEXT -- TWO DISTINCT KNOBS, DO NOT PUT THEM ON ONE AXIS. CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=8 bounds BUILD width; nextest -j bounds TEST concurrency. The 1.93x is attributable to -j alone; the wrapper's build width was unchanged throughout. Plotting a build width and a test concurrency on one axis produces a smooth curve that means nothing. Those figures are WARM-REPEAT times, not node times: this node takes 57.6s in a full validate. The SPEEDUP transfers, the absolute seconds do not. NOT ESTABLISHED: whether the serialization is still necessary. No commit or comment names which lib/bin test requires it. Two -j16 runs returned rc=0 and that is NOT evidence of safety, because a race that only appears under concurrency is exactly what two green runs cannot rule out. Raising this needs someone to confirm Hermit's lib and bin targets are free of the process-local mutex constraint; until then the 1.93x is not claimable."########,

THIS DOES NOT WEAKEN THE TEST. Where it cannot, this node used to FAIL in 0.11 s with exit 101 and an empty detail block -- indistinguishable from a broken build -- and its eager-exit aborted the twelve other in-flight nodes and filtered twenty-seven more, so a machine that runs 31 of 33 nodes in 3m22s produced no receipt at all. `requires_host_capability` moves that judgement OUT of the node, to an out-of-band probe run during plan construction, and makes the outcome a third recorded state: host-inapplicable, which is neither a pass nor a failure and is written to the ledger as a typed intentional skip. THIS DOES NOT WEAKEN THE TEST. Where the capability is present the node runs unchanged and its assertions keep full force; the probe fails closed toward running, so a probe error or an unexpected errno still runs it. The capability name is checked against the closed vocabulary in scripts/lib/validate_plan.rs::HostCapability, and an unknown name refuses the whole run."########,

THIS DOES NOT WEAKEN THE TEST.
